It was the third consecutive rate cut, bringing the policy rate to its lowest level since 2022. Policymakers noted that inflation had moderated, with the current rate of inflation below the midpoint of the 1%–3% target range, helped by a strong shekel against the dollar. It was the third consecutive rate cut, bringing the policy rate to its lowest level since 2022.
